[7m[0m    "tag",      # bare/-l/--list = read; -d/--delete = write[0m
[7m[0m    "config",   # --get/--list = read; --unset/--remove = write[0m
[7m[0m    "remote",   # bare/-v = read; add/remove/rename/set-url = write[0m
[7m[0m    "stash",    # list/show = read; push/pop/drop = write[0m
[7m[0m})[0m
[7m[0m
[7m[0m# Always-deny commands regardless of args (irreversible / network / mutating)[0m
[7m[0m_ALWAYS_DENY = frozenset({[0m
[7m[0m    "rm", "rmdir", "mv", "cp", "ln", "mkdir", "touch", "chmod", "chown", "chgrp",[0m
[7m[0m    "dd", "mkfs", "fdisk", "format", "del", "erase",[0m
[7m[0m    "curl", "wget", "scp", "rsync", "ftp", "sftp", "ssh", "telnet", "nc", "netcat",[0m
[7m [0m[7m [0m[7m [0m[7m [0m[7m[0m"pip", [0m[7m[0m"npm", [0m[7m[0m"yarn", [0m[7m[0m"gem", [0m[7m[0m"cargo", [0m[7m[0m"go", [0m[7m [0m[7m[0m# [0m[7m[0mpackage [0m[7m[0minstallers [0m[7m[0m(note: [0m[7m[0m`go` [0m[7m[0mmay [0m[7m[0malso [0m[7m[0mbuild/test [0m[7m[0m— [0m[7m[0mbare [0m
[7m[0mcommand-name [0m[7m[0mdeny)[0m
[7m[0m    "pkill", "kill", "killall",[0m
[7m[0m    "shutdown", "reboot", "halt", "poweroff", "init",[0m
[7m[0m    "sudo", "doas", "su", "runas",[0m
[7m[0m})[0m
[7m[0m
[7m[0m# Codex deny tokens — token-based check (regex \b fails on --flag boundaries[0m
[7m[0m# because \b requires word/non-word transition; `--` is non-word so no boundary[0m
[7m[0m# triggers. shlex.split + token equality is the SOTA security pattern).[0m
[7m[0m#[0m
[7m[0m# Sandbox-escalation closure (Stop hook 2026-05-03): added --sandbox / --config /[0m
[7m[0m# -c / --profile to close the runtime-override class. Even with `-p deep-review`,[0m
[7m[0m# `codex exec -p deep-review --sandbox danger-full-access` overrides profile at[0m
[7m[0m# runtime. Per codex-cli-flag-positioning.md matrix row 11: `-c key=value` is[0m
[7m[0m# global on `codex exec` and accepts arbitrary config including sandbox.mode.[0m
[7m[0m_CODEX_DENY_TOKENS = frozenset({[0m
[7m[0m    "--write",[0m
[7m[0m    "--dangerously-bypass-approvals-and-sandbox",[0m
[7m[0m    "--yolo",[0m
[7m[0m    "--sandbox",         # `--sandbox danger-full-access` overrides profile sandbox[0m
[7m[0m    "--config",          # `--config <toml>` loads alternative config[0m
[7m[0m    "-c",                # `-c key=value` inline config override (e.g. sandbox.mode=...)[0m
[7m[0m    "--profile",         # alias for -p — must be explicitly checked[0m
[7m[0m    "--full-auto",       # codex flag — auto-approve all actions[0m
[7m[0m    "--ask-for-approval",  # also accepts danger-full-access value[0m
[7m[0m    "-a",                # short form of --ask-for-approval[0m
[7m[0m    "--ignore-rules",        # bypasses execpolicy `.rules` files (codex T1 F-001 conf=0.91)[0m
[7m[0m    "--ignore-user-config",  # bypasses $CODEX_HOME/config.toml (codex T1 F-002 conf=0.91)[0m
[7m[0m})[0m
[7m[0m
[7m[0m# Codex deny token PREFIXES — match attached/equals forms that token-equality misses[0m
[7m[0m# (codex T1 F-003 residual hardening): `--config=key=value` (long-form-with-equals) and[0m
[7m[0m# `-cKEY=VALUE` (short-form-no-space) bypass exact-token deny check at L411-412.[0m
[7m[0m_CODEX_DENY_TOKEN_PREFIXES = ([0m
[7m[0m    "--config=",  # `--config=approval_policy=never` etc[0m
[7m[0m    "-c",         # `-csandbox_mode=danger-full-access` (no-space short form);[0m
[7m[0m                  # narrower than allowing bare `-c` because exact `-c` is in DENY_TOKENS[0m
[7m[0m                  # above, this catches `-c<value>` attached forms only[0m
[7m[0m)[0m
[7m[0m# Codex deny substrings — match anywhere in any token (catches `-p deep-review-exec`)[0m
[7m[0m_CODEX_DENY_SUBSTRINGS = ([0m
[7m[0m    "deep-review-exec",       # danger-full-access profile[0m
[7m[0m    "danger-full-access",     # raw sandbox value[0m
[7m[0m    "danger-bypass",          # alternative dangerous value[0m
[7m[0m    "workspace-write",        # write-capable sandbox[0m
[7m[0m    "no-sandbox",             # disabling sandbox[0m
[7m[0m)[0m
[7m[0m
[7m[0m# Sed mutation + subprocess-spawning patterns (Stop-hook fix-forward round-2 2026-05-03)[0m
[7m[0m# - `sed -i` / `--in-place`: file write[0m
[7m[0m# - `sed 's/foo/bar/e'`: e-flag executes replacement as shell command[0m
[7m[0m# - `sed '1e cmd'` / `sed 'e cmd'`: e command evaluates pattern space as shell[0m
[7m[0m# Read-only mode rejects ALL sed forms that can write OR spawn subprocesses.[0m
[7m[0m_SED_DENY_PATTERNS = ([0m
[7m[0m    re.compile(r"^sed\s+(?:.*\s+)?-i\b", re.IGNORECASE),[0m
[7m[0m    re.compile(r"^sed\s+(?:.*\s+)?--in-place\b", re.IGNORECASE),[0m
[7m[0m    # s<sep>...<sep>...<sep><flags-with-e>  — substitute with e flag (executes[0m
[7m[0m    # replacement as shell). Uses backref \1 to support arbitrary separators[0m
[7m[0m    # (/, |, :, ,, #, ~ — sed accepts any non-alphanumeric non-whitespace char).[0m
[7m[0m    # `[\s\S]` matches any char including newline (DOTALL semantics inline).[0m
[7m[0m    re.compile(r"\bs(.)(?:(?!\1)[\s\S])*?\1(?:(?!\1)[\s\S])*?\1[gimpI0-9]*e", re.IGNORECASE),[0m
[7m[0m    # Bare e command: at start of segment, after ;, after !, after { (sed[0m
[7m[0m    # control structures), OR after ' / " (start of sed script literal in shell).[0m
[7m[0m    # The `e` command followed by whitespace+arg evaluates the arg as shell.[0m
[7m[0m    re.compile(r"(?:^|[;!{'\"])\s*\d*\s*e\s+\S", re.IGNORECASE),[0m
[7m[0m)[0m
[7m[0m
[7m[0m# Awk subprocess-spawning patterns (Stop-hook fix-forward round-2 2026-05-03)[0m
[7m[0m# Awk is Turing-complete with built-in subprocess primitives that bypass the[0m
[7m[0m# leading-verb allowlist:[0m
[7m[0m# - `system("cmd")`            — direct subprocess execution[0m
[7m[0m# - `print ... | "cmd"`        — pipe output to subprocess (awk-internal `|`,[0m
[7m[0m#                                 distinct from shell `|` so _COMMAND_SEPARATORS misses it)[0m
[7m[0m# - `"cmd" | getline`          — pipe input from subprocess[0m
[7m[0m# - `getline var | "cmd"`      — coprocess-style two-way pipe[0m
[7m[0m# - `awk -f script.awk`        — read script from external file (uninspectable)[0m
[7m[0m# - `@load "extension"`        — gawk dynamic extension loading[0m
[7m[0m# Read-only mode rejects ALL these; legitimate read-only awk uses print/printf/match[0m
[7m[0m# without subprocess spawning.[0m
[7m[0m_AWK_SUBPROCESS_DENY_PATTERNS = ([0m
[7m[0m    re.compile(r"\bsystem\s*\("),                # system("cmd") — direct subprocess[0m
[7m[0m    re.compile(r"\|\s*[\"']"),                    # | "cmd" or | 'cmd' — pipe to subprocess[0m
[7m[0m    re.compile(r"[\"']\s*\|\s*getline\b"),        # "cmd" | getline — pipe from subprocess[0m
[7m[0m    re.compile(r"\bgetline\s+\w*\s*\|"),          # getline var | — coprocess form[0m
[7m[0m    re.compile(r"@load\b"),                       # gawk dynamic extension loading[0m
[7m[0m)[0m
[7m[0m# Awk option flags that bypass script inspection (load script from external file)[0m
[7m[0m_AWK_DENY_FLAGS = frozenset({[0m
[7m[0m    "-f", "--file",       # read script from file (uninspectable)[0m
[7m[0m    "-i", "--include",    # gawk include directive[0m
[7m[0m    "-l", "--load",       # gawk extension load[0m
[7m[0m    "-e", "--source",     # NB: -e ALSO valid for inline script in some awks; conservative deny[0m
[7m[0m})[0m
[7m[0m
[7m[0m# Shell write redirections (>, >>) — detect at command-string level[0m
[7m[0m_REDIRECT_WRITE_PATTERN = re.compile(r"(?<![<>&])(?:>>?)\s*\S")[0m
[7m[0m
[7m[0m# Stop-hook fix-forward 2026-05-03 — close shell-substitution bypass class.[0m
[7m[0m# The shell evaluates substitutions BEFORE the leading verb runs, so a[0m
[7m[0m# leading-verb allowlist that only inspects `git`/`ls`/`cat` etc. is bypassed[0m
[7m[0m# when the inner substitution itself runs an arbitrary command at exec time[0m
[7m[0m# (e.g. `git diff $(rm -rf /)` allowlists `git diff` but executes the inner[0m
[7m[0m# command at substitution time before `git diff` even starts).[0m
[7m[0m#[0m
[7m[0m# Empirical confirmation: tmp/probe_substitution_bypass.py 6/11 bypass cases[0m
[7m[0m# under prior code (n=6 distinct bypass shapes — $(...), backtick, <(...),[0m
[7m[0m# ${...}, $((...)), newline-separator).[0m
[7m[0m#[0m
[7m[0m# Read-only mode has no legitimate need for command substitution, backticks,[0m
[7m[0m# process substitution, or parameter expansion with embedded commands; HARD[0m
[7m[0m# DENY is the kiss-dry-yagni-aligned closure (fail-closed per[0m
[7m[0m# security-checklist.md posture + layered-gates-architecture.md §13 anti-[0m
[7m[0m# pattern "Try to enforce at front gate via async: true alone").[0m
[7m[0m#[0m
[7m[0m# NB: \$\( catches BOTH `$(...)` command substitution AND `$((...))` arithmetic[0m
[7m[0m# substitution (both share `$(` prefix). One pattern, two coverages.[0m
[7m[0m_SHELL_SUBSTITUTION_DENY_PATTERNS = ([0m
[7m[0m    re.compile(r"\$\("),  # $(...) command sub + $((...)) arithmetic sub[0m
[7m[0m    re.compile(r"`"),     # `...`  backtick command substitution[0m
[7m[0m    re.compile(r"<\("),   # <(...) process substitution (input)[0m
[7m[0m    re.compile(r">\("),   # >(...) process substitution (output)[0m
[7m[0m    re.compile(r"\$\{"),  # ${...} parameter expansion (may embed cmd via :-$(cmd))[0m
[7m[0m)[0m
[7m[0m
[7m[0m# Newline is a Bash command separator that _COMMAND_SEPARATORS misses.[0m
[7m[0m# `git status\nrm -rf /` would only validate the first segment after split.[0m
[7m[0m# Read-only mode rejects multiline commands; one command per Bash invocation suffices.[0m
[7m[0m_NEWLINE_SEPARATOR_PATTERN = re.compile(r"[\r\n]")[0m
[7m[0m
[7m[0m
[7m[0mdef _split_segments(cmd: str) -> list[str]:[0m
[7m[0m    """Split a Bash string by UNQUOTED command separators into individual command segments.[0m
[7m[0m
[7m[0m    Implements the same per-segment-validation contract that the removed[0m
[7m[0m    bash_command_allowlist.py:split_command_segments helper used to provide before[0m
[7m[0m    Wave 11A removal. Each segment is a single command + its args. Compound commands[0m
[7m[0m    (a && b ; c) are split into [a, b, c] for individual validation.[0m
[7m[0m
[7m[0m    Quote-aware: separators inside single ('...') or double ("...") quotes are NOT[0m
[7m[0m    split. This is critical for `python -c 'import sys; print(sys.version)'` which[0m
[7m[0m    contains a literal ';' inside the quoted argument.[0m
[7m[0m    """[0m
[7m[0m    segments: list[str] = [][0m
[7m[0m    current: list[str] = [][0m
[7m[0m    i = 0[0m
[7m[0m    in_single = False[0m
[7m[0m    in_double = False[0m
[7m[0m    while i < len(cmd):[0m
[7m[0m        c = cmd[i][0m
[7m[0m        # Quote state tracking[0m
[7m[0m        if c == "'" and not in_double:[0m
[7m[0m            in_single = not in_single[0m
[7m[0m            current.append(c)[0m
[7m[0m            i += 1[0m
[7m[0m            continue[0m
[7m[0m        if c == '"' and not in_single:[0m
[7m[0m            in_double = not in_double[0m
[7m[0m            current.append(c)[0m
[7m[0m            i += 1[0m
[7m[0m            continue[0m
[7m[0m        # Only split on separators OUTSIDE quotes[0m
[7m[0m        if not in_single and not in_double:[0m
[7m[0m            # Multi-char separators first (&& ||)[0m
[7m[0m            if cmd[i : i + 2] in ("&&", "||"):[0m
[7m[0m                segments.append("".join(current).strip())[0m
[7m[0m                current = [][0m
[7m[0m                i += 2[0m
[7m[0m                continue[0m
[7m[0m            # Single-char separators (; | &)[0m
[7m[0m            # `&` is bash background-execution separator: `cmd1 & cmd2` runs cmd1 in[0m
[7m[0m            # background AND cmd2 in foreground. Without splitting, leading-token[0m
[7m[0m            # allowlist on cmd1 lets cmd2 (potentially destructive) execute unchecked.[0m
[7m[0m            # Codex T1 F-004 conf=0.91 HIGH: `git status & rm -rf /` was accepted as[0m
[7m[0m            # single segment with `git` allowlisted. Multi-char `&&` handled at L266-270[0m
[7m[0m            # ABOVE this single-char check (precedence preserved by ordering).[0m
[7m[0m            if c in (";", "|", "&"):[0m
[7m[0m                segments.append("".join(current).strip())[0m
[7m[0m                current = [][0m
[7m[0m                i += 1[0m
[7m[0m                continue[0m
[7m[0m        current.append(c)[0m
[7m[0m        i += 1[0m
[7m[0m    # Append final segment[0m
[7m[0m    tail = "".join(current).strip()[0m
[7m[0m    if tail:[0m
[7m[0m        segments.append(tail)[0m
[7m[0m    return [s for s in segments if s][0m
[7m[0m
[7m[0m
[7m[0mdef _first_token(segment: str) -> str:[0m
[7m[0m    """Extract the first token (command name) from a segment, stripping basename + extension."""[0m
[7m[0m    try:[0m
[7m[0m        tokens = shlex.split(segment)[0m
[7m[0m    except ValueError:[0m
[7m[0m        # shlex can't parse (unmatched quote etc) — return raw split as fallback[0m
[7m[0m        tokens = segment.split()[0m
[7m[0m    if not tokens:[0m
[7m[0m        return ""[0m
[7m[0m    # Get basename without extension (e.g. /usr/bin/python3.11 -> python)[0m
[7m[0m    cmd = os.path.basename(tokens[0]).lower()[0m
[7m[0m    # Strip common executable extensions[0m
[7m[0m    for ext in (".exe", ".cmd", ".bat", ".ps1"):[0m
[7m[0m        if cmd.endswith(ext):[0m
[7m[0m            cmd = cmd[: -len(ext)][0m
[7m[0m            break[0m
[7m[0m    return cmd[0m
[7m[0m
[7m[0m
[7m[0mdef _validate_git_ambiguous_subcmd(subcmd: str, tokens: list[str], i: int) -> tuple[bool, str]:[0m
[7m[0m    """Validate ambiguous git subcommands (branch/tag/config/remote/stash) by inspecting flags.[0m
[7m[0m
[7m[0m    Bare invocation (no flag args) defaults to READ for these (they list/show by default).[0m
[7m[0m    Specific write flags trigger DENY.[0m
[7m[0m    """[0m
[7m[0m    next_arg = tokens[i + 1] if i + 1 < len(tokens) else ""[0m
[7m[0m
[7m[0m    if subcmd == "stash":[0m
[7m[0m        # bare `git stash` = `git stash push` (write); list/show = read[0m
[7m[0m        if next_arg in {"list", "show"}:[0m
[7m[0m            return True, "allow:git stash list/show is read-only"[0m
[7m[0m        return False, f"block:git stash {next_arg or '(bare = push)'} is write"[0m
[7m[0m
[7m[0m    if subcmd == "tag":[0m
[7m[0m        if next_arg in {"-l", "--list", ""}:[0m
[7m[0m            return True, "allow:git tag --list/bare is read-only"[0m
[7m[0m        if next_arg in {"-d", "-D", "--delete"}:[0m
[7m[0m            return False, "block:git tag --delete is write"[0m
[7m[0m        # Creating a tag (e.g. `git tag v1.0`) is write[0m
[7m[0m        return False, f"block:git tag {next_arg} (create) is write"[0m
[7m[0m
[7m[0m    if subcmd == "config":[0m
[7m[0m        if next_arg in {"--get", "--list", "-l", "--get-all", "--get-regexp", "--show-origin"}:[0m
[7m[0m            return True, "allow:git config --get/--list is read-only"[0m
[7m[0m        if next_arg in {"--unset", "--unset-all", "--remove-section", "--rename-section"}:[0m
[7m[0m            return False, "block:git config --unset/--remove is write"[0m
[7m[0m        # Bare `git config foo.bar` (read single) is allowed; `git config foo.bar value` (set) is write[0m
[7m[0m        # Heuristic: 1 positional after subcmd = read; 2+ positionals = write[0m
[7m[0m        positionals = [t for t in tokens[i + 1 :] if not t.startswith("-")][0m
[7m[0m        if len(positionals) <= 1:[0m
[7m[0m            return True, "allow:git config single positional is read"[0m
[7m[0m        return False, "block:git config with value (set) is write"[0m
[7m[0m
[7m[0m    if subcmd == "branch":[0m
[7m[0m        if next_arg in {"", "-l", "--list", "-a", "-r", "--all", "--remotes", "-v", "-vv", "--verbose"}:[0m
[7m[0m            return True, "allow:git branch list/bare is read-only"[0m
[7m[0m        if next_arg in {"-d", "-D", "--delete"}:[0m
[7m[0m            return False, "block:git branch --delete is write"[0m
[7m[0m        if next_arg in {"-m", "-M", "--move"}:[0m
[7m[0m            return False, "block:git branch --move is write"[0m
[7m[0m        if next_arg in {"-c", "-C", "--copy"}:[0m
[7m[0m            return False, "block:git branch --copy is write"[0m
[7m[0m        # Creating a branch (e.g. `git branch new-feature`) is write[0m
[7m[0m        return False, f"block:git branch {next_arg} (create) is write"[0m
[7m[0m
[7m[0m    if subcmd == "remote":[0m
[7m[0m        if next_arg in {"", "-v", "--verbose", "show", "get-url"}:[0m
[7m[0m            return True, "allow:git remote bare/-v/show is read-only"[0m
[7m[0m        if next_arg in {"add", "remove", "rename", "set-url", "set-head", "prune", "rm", "set-branches"}:[0m
[7m[0m            return False, f"block:git remote {next_arg} is write"[0m
[7m[0m        return False, f"block:git remote {next_arg} unknown subcommand (fail-closed)"[0m
[7m[0m
[7m[0m    return False, f"block:git {subcmd} ambiguous subcommand fall-through (should not reach)"[0m
[7m[0m
[7m[0m
[7m[0mdef _validate_git(segment: str) -> tuple[bool, str]:[0m
[7m[0m    """Validate `git <subcmd>` — allow read subcommands, deny write subcommands."""[0m
[7m[0m    try:[0m
[7m[0m        tokens = shlex.split(segment)[0m
[7m[0m    except ValueError:[0m
[7m[0m        return False, "block:git unparseable"[0m
[7m[0m    # Skip `git` and any global flags like `-C path`, `--git-dir=...`, `--work-tree=...`[0m
[7m[0m    i = 1[0m
[7m[0m    while i < len(tokens):[0m
[7m[0m        t = tokens[i][0m
[7m[0m        if t.startswith("--") or t == "-C":[0m
[7m[0m            # Skip -C and its arg, or --flag=value[0m
[7m[0m            i += 2 if t == "-C" else 1[0m
[7m[0m            continue[0m
[7m[0m        if t.startswith("-"):[0m
[7m[0m            i += 1[0m
[7m[0m            continue[0m
[7m[0m        break[0m
[7m[0m    if i >= len(tokens):[0m
[7m[0m        return True, "allow:bare git command (e.g. `git`)"[0m
[7m[0m    subcmd = tokens[i].lower()[0m
[7m[0m    # Ambiguous subcommands (read or write depending on flags) — check FIRST[0m
[7m[0m    if subcmd in _GIT_AMBIGUOUS_SUBCMDS:[0m
[7m[0m        return _validate_git_ambiguous_subcmd(subcmd, tokens, i)[0m
[7m[0m    # Pure write subcommands[0m
[7m[0m    if subcmd in _GIT_WRITE_SUBCMDS:[0m
[7m[0m        return False, f"block:git {subcmd} is write subcommand"[0m
[7m[0m    # Pure read subcommands[0m
[7m[0m    if subcmd in _GIT_READ_SUBCMDS:[0m
[7m[0m        return True, f"allow:git {subcmd} is read-only"[0m
[7m[0m    # Unknown subcommand — fail closed (per security-checklist.md fail-closed posture)[0m
[7m[0m    return False, f"block:git {subcmd} unknown subcommand (fail-closed)"[0m
[7m[0m
[7m[0m
[7m[0mdef _validate_codex(segment: str, policy: str) -> tuple[bool, str]:[0m
[7m[0m    """Validate `codex ...` invocation per policy mode (token-based for boundary safety).[0m
[7m[0m
[7m[0m    Regex `\\b--word\\b` fails to match `--word` because `\\b` requires word/non-word[0m
[7m[0m    transition; `--` is non-word so no boundary triggers. Token-based check via shlex.split[0m
[7m[0m    is the SOTA-recommended fix per security-checklist allowlist patterns.[0m
[7m[0m    """[0m
[7m[0m    if policy != "codex-readonly":[0m
[7m[0m        return False, "block:codex invocation requires --policy codex-readonly"[0m
[7m[0m    try:[0m
[7m[0m        tokens = shlex.split(segment)[0m
[7m[0m    except ValueError:[0m
[7m[0m        return False, "block:codex unparseable"[0m
[7m[0m
[7m[0m    # Token-based deny check (closes \b regex boundary issue)[0m
[7m[0m    for tok in tokens:[0m
[7m[0m        if tok in _CODEX_DENY_TOKENS:[0m
[7m[0m            return False, f"block:codex deny-token {tok}"[0m
[7m[0m        # Prefix check for attached/equals forms (codex T1 F-003 residual hardening):[0m
[7m[0m        # `--config=key=value` and `-cKEY=VALUE` bypass exact-token deny above.[0m
[7m[0m        # Skip bare exact matches already in DENY_TOKENS to avoid duplicate-error noise.[0m
[7m[0m        for prefix in _CODEX_DENY_TOKEN_PREFIXES:[0m
[7m[0m            if tok.startswith(prefix) and tok != prefix and tok not in _CODEX_DENY_TOKENS:[0m
[7m[0m                return False, f"block:codex deny-prefix {prefix} in token {tok}"[0m
[7m[0m        # Substring check for embedded patterns (e.g. -p deep-review-exec)[0m
[7m[0m        for sub in _CODEX_DENY_SUBSTRINGS:[0m
[7m[0m            if sub in tok.lower():[0m
[7m[0m                return False, f"block:codex deny-substring {sub} in token {tok}"[0m
[7m[0m
[7m[0m    # Structural allow check: require specific subcommand shape[0m
[7m[0m    # Acceptable forms:[0m
[7m[0m    #   codex --version | -V[0m
[7m[0m    #   codex --help | -h[0m
[7m[0m    #   codex exec --help | -h[0m
[7m[0m    #   codex exec [--ephemeral] -p deep-review [...]            (+ allowed extras)[0m
[7m[0m    #   codex exec review --uncommitted [--ephemeral] [...][0m
[7m[0m    if len(tokens) < 2:[0m
[7m[0m        return False, "block:bare codex invocation"[0m
[7m[0m
[7m[0m    # `codex --version` / `codex --help`[0m
[7m[0m    if tokens[1] in {"--version", "-V", "--help", "-h"}:[0m
[7m[0m        return True, f"allow:codex {tokens[1]}"[0m
[7m[0m
[7m[0m    # All allowed forms after `codex` start with `exec`[0m
[7m[0m    if tokens[1] != "exec":[0m
[7m[0m        return False, f"block:codex subcommand '{tokens[1]}' not in readonly allowlist"[0m
[7m[0m
[7m[0m    # `codex exec --help` / `codex exec -h`[0m
[7m[0m    if len(tokens) >= 3 and tokens[2] in {"--help", "-h"}:[0m
[7m[0m        return True, "allow:codex exec --help"[0m
[7m[0m
[7m[0m    # `codex exec review --uncommitted ...` (review subcommand)[0m
[7m[0m    if len(tokens) >= 3 and tokens[2] == "review":[0m
[7m[0m        if "--uncommitted" not in tokens:[0m
[7m[0m            return False, "block:codex exec review requires --uncommitted in readonly mode"[0m
[7m[0m        # All tokens after `review` must be in safe set[0m
[7m[0m        return True, "allow:codex exec review --uncommitted"[0m
[7m[0m
[7m[0m    # `codex exec [...] -p deep-review [...]` — REQUIRES explicit -p deep-review[0m
[7m[0m    # Sandbox-escalation closure (Stop hook 2026-05-03):[0m
[7m[0m    # - `codex exec --ephemeral` WITHOUT -p was a bypass: default profile is operator-[0m
[7m[0m    #   configurable and could be `deep-review-exec` (danger-full-access).[0m
[7m[0m    # - `codex exec -p deep-review --sandbox X` overrides profile sandbox; closed by[0m
[7m[0m    #   _CODEX_DENY_TOKENS containing --sandbox.[0m
[7m[0m    # - `codex exec -p deep-review -c sandbox.mode=X` overrides via inline config;[0m
[7m[0m    #   closed by _CODEX_DENY_TOKENS containing -c / --config.[0m
[7m[0m    # The strict requirement: explicit `-p deep-review` is the ONLY allowed exec form.[0m
[7m[0m    if "-p" not in tokens:[0m
[7m [0m[7m [0m[7m [0m[7m [0m[7m [0m[7m [0m[7m [0m[7m [0m[7m[0mreturn [0m[7m[0mFalse, [0m[7m[0m"block:codex [0m[7m[0mexec [0m[7m[0mrequires [0m[7m[0mexplicit [0m[7m[0m-p [0m[7m[0mdeep-review [0m[7m[0m(default [0m[7m[0mprofile [0m[7m[0mis [0m[7m[0moperator-configurable, [0m
[7m[0mnot [0m[7m[0msafe [0m[7m[0mto [0m[7m[0massume [0m[7m[0mreadonly)"[0m
[7m[0m    p_idx = tokens.index("-p")[0m
[7m[0m    if p_idx + 1 >= len(tokens):[0m
[7m[0m        return False, "block:codex -p missing profile name"[0m
[7m[0m    profile = tokens[p_idx + 1][0m
[7m[0m    if profile != "deep-review":[0m
[7m[0m        return False, f"block:codex -p {profile} not in readonly allowlist (only deep-review)"[0m
[7m[0m    return True, "allow:codex exec -p deep-review"[0m
[7m[0m
[7m[0m
[7m[0mdef _validate_sed(segment: str) -> tuple[bool, str]:[0m
[7m[0m    """sed -i / --in-place is write; sed `e` flag/command spawns subprocess; -n print is read."""[0m
[7m[0m    for pat in _SED_DENY_PATTERNS:[0m
[7m[0m        if pat.search(segment):[0m
[7m[0m            # Distinguish reasons for forensic clarity[0m
[7m[0m            ps = pat.pattern[0m
[7m[0m            if "in-place" in ps or r"-i\b" in ps:[0m
[7m[0m                return False, "block:sed -i/--in-place writes to file"[0m
[7m[0m            if "e[gimpI" in ps or "0-9]*e" in ps:[0m
[7m[0m                return False, "block:sed s///e flag executes replacement as shell (subprocess spawn)"[0m
[7m[0m            if r"e\s+\S" in ps:[0m
[7m[0m                return False, "block:sed `e` command evaluates pattern space as shell (subprocess spawn)"[0m
[7m[0m            return False, f"block:sed deny pattern ({ps})"[0m
[7m[0m    return True, "allow:sed without -i/--in-place/e-flag is read-only"[0m
[7m[0m
[7m[0m
[7m[0mdef _validate_awk(segment: str) -> tuple[bool, str]:[0m
[7m[0m    """awk subprocess-spawning is HARD DENY (system, pipe-to-cmd, pipe-from-cmd, getline-coproc).[0m
[7m[0m
[7m[0m    Stop-hook fix-forward round-2 2026-05-03 — awk's Turing-complete language has[0m
[7m[0m    multiple subprocess primitives that bypass the leading-verb allowlist. Read-only[0m
[7m[0m    mode rejects them all; legitimate read-only awk uses print/printf/match/etc.[0m
[7m[0m    without subprocess spawning. Also rejects `-f script.awk` (uninspectable file).[0m
[7m[0m    """[0m
[7m[0m    # Tokenize for flag inspection[0m
[7m[0m    try:[0m
[7m[0m        tokens = shlex.split(segment)[0m
[7m[0m    except ValueError:[0m
[7m[0m        # Unparseable — fail-closed (likely contains injected metacharacters)[0m
[7m[0m        return False, "block:awk segment unparseable (fail-closed)"[0m
[7m[0m
[7m[0m    # Reject awk option flags that bypass script inspection (read script from file)[0m
[7m[0m    for tok in tokens[1:]:[0m
[7m[0m        # Match flag itself or flag=value form (e.g. --file=script.awk)[0m
[7m[0m        flag = tok.split("=", 1)[0] if "=" in tok else tok[0m
[7m[0m        if flag in _AWK_DENY_FLAGS:[0m
[7m[0m            return False, ([0m
[7m[0m                f"block:awk {tok} reads script from external source (uninspectable); "[0m
[7m[0m                "read-only mode requires inline script literal"[0m
[7m[0m            )[0m
[7m[0m
[7m[0m    # Reject subprocess-spawning patterns inside awk script string[0m
[7m[0m    for pat in _AWK_SUBPROCESS_DENY_PATTERNS:[0m
[7m[0m        if pat.search(segment):[0m
[7m[0m            return False, ([0m
[7m[0m                f"block:awk subprocess pattern ({pat.pattern}) — awk system()/pipe-to/"[0m
[7m[0m                "pipe-from/coprocess/extension-load bypass leading-verb allowlist"[0m
[7m[0m            )[0m
[7m[0m
[7m[0m    return True, "allow:awk without system()/pipe/coprocess is read-only"[0m
[7m[0m
[7m[0m
[7m[0mdef _validate_find(segment: str) -> tuple[bool, str]:[0m
[7m[0m    """find with -delete / -exec / -execdir is write; bare find or find -type is read."""[0m
[7m[0m    if re.search(r"-delete\b", segment):[0m
[7m[0m        return False, "block:find -delete is write"[0m
[7m[0m    if re.search(r"-exec(?:dir)?\b", segment):[0m
[7m[0m        return False, "block:find -exec/-execdir spawns subprocesses (deny by default)"[0m
[7m[0m    if re.search(r"-fprint\b", segment):[0m
[7m[0m        return False, "block:find -fprint is write"[0m
[7m[0m    return True, "allow:find without -delete/-exec is read-only"[0m
[7m[0m
[7m[0m
[7m[0mdef _validate_python(segment: str) -> tuple[bool, str]:[0m
[7m[0m    """python -c with limited surface; python script.py blocked (could be anything)."""[0m
[7m[0m    try:[0m
[7m[0m        tokens = shlex.split(segment)[0m
[7m[0m    except ValueError:[0m
[7m[0m        return False, "block:python unparseable"[0m
[7m[0m    if len(tokens) < 2:[0m
[7m[0m        return False, "block:bare python invocation (interactive REPL not supported in subagent)"[0m
[7m[0m    # Allow python --version / python -V / python -h / python --help[0m
[7m[0m    if tokens[1] in {"--version", "-V", "-h", "--help"}:[0m
[7m[0m        return True, f"allow:python {tokens[1]} is read-only"[0m
[7m[0m    # Block python -m pip install / etc.[0m
[7m[0m    if tokens[1] == "-m" and len(tokens) > 2 and tokens[2] in {"pip", "ensurepip", "venv"}:[0m
[7m[0m        return False, f"block:python -m {tokens[2]} is package management"[0m
[7m[0m    # Allow python -c with no obvious mutation keywords[0m
[7m[0m    if tokens[1] == "-c" and len(tokens) > 2:[0m
[7m[0m        code = tokens[2][0m
[7m[0m        # Conservative deny of mutation patterns[0m
[7m[0m        deny_patterns = ("open(", "os.system", "os.remove", "os.unlink", "os.rmdir",[0m
[7m[0m                          "shutil.rm", "subprocess", "Popen", "exec(", "eval(",[0m
[7m[0m                          "import os.system", "__import__", "compile(")[0m
[7m[0m        for dp in deny_patterns:[0m
[7m[0m            if dp in code:[0m
[7m[0m                return False, f"block:python -c contains mutation pattern ({dp})"[0m
[7m[0m        return True, "allow:python -c with no mutation patterns"[0m
[7m[0m    return False, "block:python with unknown args (fail-closed)"[0m
[7m[0m
[7m[0m
[7m[0mdef _validate_verifier_pytest(segment: str) -> tuple[bool, str]:[0m
[7m[0m    """Verifier policy: allow `pytest` with safe args; deny `-c` (Python eval) + bad --basetemp."""[0m
[7m[0m    try:[0m
[7m[0m        tokens = shlex.split(segment, posix=False)[0m
[7m[0m    except ValueError:[0m
[7m[0m        return False, "block:pytest command parsing failed"[0m
[7m[0m    for i, t in enumerate(tokens):[0m
[7m[0m        if t == "-c":[0m
[7m[0m            return False, "block:pytest -c (arbitrary Python eval) denied under verifier policy"[0m
[7m[0m        # --basetemp escape: deny ALL paths that escape cwd, not just absolute paths.[0m
[7m[0m        # Three bypass classes closed (Stop hook block #5, 2026-05-03):[0m
[7m[0m        #   (1) Absolute paths: /tmp/evil, \evil, C:\evil — original check[0m
[7m[0m        #   (2) Tilde expansion: ~/evil, ~user/evil — shell expands to abs before pytest sees[0m
[7m[0m        #   (3) Path traversal: ../../../tmp/evil, ./../../tmp, subdir/../../tmp —[0m
[7m[0m        #       relative paths whose normpath escapes cwd via `..` segments[0m
[7m[0m        # Verifier writes only to worktree-relative test session dirs that DESCEND from cwd.[0m
[7m[0m        bt = None[0m
[7m[0m        if t == "--basetemp" and i + 1 < len(tokens):[0m
[7m[0m            bt = tokens[i + 1][0m
[7m[0m        elif t.startswith("--basetemp="):[0m
[7m[0m            bt = t.split("=", 1)[1][0m
[7m[0m        if bt:[0m
[7m[0m            # Stop hook block #5 fix-forward (2026-05-03): strip outer matched quotes.[0m
[7m[0m            # shlex.split(posix=False) at L589 PRESERVES literal quote chars in tokens,[0m
[7m[0m            # so `pytest --basetemp "/tmp/evil"` yields bt=`"/tmp/evil"` (with quotes).[0m
[7m[0m            # The absolute-path check at L611 sees first char `"` not `/` and bypasses.[0m
[7m[0m            # Strip outer matched single/double quotes BEFORE path-class checks.[0m
[7m[0m            if len(bt) >= 2 and bt[0] == bt[-1] and bt[0] in ('"', "'"):[0m
[7m[0m                bt = bt[1:-1][0m
[7m[0m            # Also strip equals-form bt that may carry quotes after =, e.g. --basetemp="..."[0m
[7m[0m            # (handled by the same outer-quote strip above)[0m
[7m[0m            # (1) Absolute paths (Unix `/`, Windows `\` or drive-letter `C:`)[0m
[7m[0m            if bt.startswith("/") or bt.startswith("\\") or (len(bt) > 1 and bt[1] == ":"):[0m
[7m[0m                return False, f"block:pytest --basetemp must be cwd-relative; absolute path rejected ({bt})"[0m
[7m[0m            # (2) Tilde expansion (shell expands ~/X and ~user/X to absolute)[0m
[7m[0m            if bt.startswith("~"):[0m
[7m[0m                return False, f"block:pytest --basetemp must not use `~` tilde expansion; rejected ({bt})"[0m
[7m[0m            # (2b) Shell-expansion sigils — bash WOULD expand $HOME, ${HOME}, $PWD, $TMPDIR,[0m
[7m[0m            #      %USERPROFILE%, `cmd` to absolute paths BEFORE pytest receives the arg.[0m
[7m[0m            #      Codex T1 NEEDS-REVISION conf=0.91 F-002 P1 verdict[0m
[7m[0m            #      (.claude/state/codex_consult_pytest_basetemp_traversal_fix_OUT.txt:2988-2990)[0m
[7m[0m            #      LIVE PROBE: `pytest --basetemp $HOME/evil => allowed=True` pre-fix.[0m
[7m[0m            #      Global denylist at L236 catches `${...}`, `$(`, backticks BUT NOT bare `$HOME`.[0m
[7m[0m            if any(sigil in bt for sigil in ("$", "%", "`")):[0m
[7m[0m                return False, f"block:pytest --basetemp must not use shell-expansion sigils ($,%,`); rejected ({bt})"[0m
[7m[0m            # (3) Path traversal via `..` segments — normpath resolves them; if result is[0m
[7m[0m            #     `..` itself OR starts with `../`, the original path escapes cwd. Sanitize[0m
[7m[0m            #     Windows `\` to `/` for cross-platform consistency. Codex T1 Q2 prescription[0m
[7m[0m            #     (.claude/state/codex_consult_pytest_basetemp_traversal_fix_OUT.txt:3045):[0m
[7m[0m            #     prefer `== ".." or startswith("../")` over `startswith("..")` to avoid[0m
[7m[0m            #     over-denying legit dirnames like `..safe/foo` (which normpath preserves).[0m
[7m[0m            bt_norm = os.path.normpath(bt).replace("\\", "/")[0m
[7m[0m            if bt_norm == ".." or bt_norm.startswith("../"):[0m
[7m [0m[7m [0m[7m [0m[7m [0m[7m [0m[7m [0m[7m [0m[7m [0m[7m [0m[7m [0m[7m [0m[7m [0m[7m [0m[7m [0m[7m [0m[7m [0m[7m[0mreturn [0m[7m[0mFalse, [0m[7m[0mf"block:pytest [0m[7m[0m--basetemp [0m[7m[0mmust [0m[7m[0mstay [0m[7m[0mwithin [0m[7m[0mcwd; [0m[7m[0m`..` [0m[7m[0mtraversal [0m[7m[0mrejected [0m[7m[0m({bt} [0m[7m[0m-> [0m
[7m[0m{bt_norm})"[0m
[7m[0m    return True, "allow:pytest with safe args (verifier policy)"[0m
[7m[0m
[7m[0m
[7m[0mdef _validate_verifier_pyright(segment: str) -> tuple[bool, str]:[0m
[7m[0m    """Verifier policy: allow `pyright`; deny `--watch` (long-running daemon)."""[0m
[7m[0m    try:[0m
[7m[0m        tokens = shlex.split(segment, posix=False)[0m
[7m[0m    except ValueError:[0m
[7m[0m        return False, "block:pyright command parsing failed"[0m
[7m[0m    for t in tokens:[0m
[7m[0m        if t == "--watch" or t.startswith("--watch="):[0m
[7m[0m            return False, "block:pyright --watch (long-running daemon) denied under verifier policy"[0m
[7m[0m    return True, "allow:pyright (verifier policy)"[0m
[7m[0m
[7m[0m
[7m[0mdef _validate_verifier_ruff(segment: str) -> tuple[bool, str]:[0m
[7m[0m    """Verifier policy: allow `ruff check` (NOT --fix) + `ruff format --check`; deny mutating forms."""[0m
[7m[0m    try:[0m
[7m[0m        tokens = shlex.split(segment, posix=False)[0m
[7m[0m    except ValueError:[0m
[7m[0m        return False, "block:ruff command parsing failed"[0m
[7m[0m    if len(tokens) < 2:[0m
[7m[0m        return False, "block:ruff requires subcommand (check or format --check)"[0m
[7m[0m    sub = tokens[1][0m
[7m[0m    if sub == "check":[0m
[7m[0m        for t in tokens[2:]:[0m
[7m[0m            if t == "--fix" or t.startswith("--fix=") or t == "--fix-only":[0m
[7m[0m                return False, "block:ruff check --fix is mutating; verifier policy denies"[0m
[7m[0m        return True, "allow:ruff check (verifier policy)"[0m
[7m[0m    if sub == "format":[0m
[7m[0m        if "--check" in tokens or "--diff" in tokens:[0m
[7m[0m            return True, "allow:ruff format --check (verifier policy)"[0m
[7m[0m        return False, "block:ruff format without --check/--diff is mutating; verifier policy denies"[0m
[7m[0m    return False, f"block:ruff subcommand '{sub}' not in verifier allowlist (check or format --check)"[0m
[7m[0m
[7m[0m
[7m[0mdef _validate_verifier_npm(segment: str) -> tuple[bool, str]:[0m
[7m[0m    """Verifier policy: allow `npm test` + `npm run {test,typecheck,lint,check}`; deny installs/builds."""[0m
[7m[0m    try:[0m
[7m[0m        tokens = shlex.split(segment, posix=False)[0m
[7m[0m    except ValueError:[0m
[7m[0m        return False, "block:npm command parsing failed"[0m
[7m[0m    if len(tokens) < 2:[0m
[7m[0m        return False, "block:npm requires subcommand"[0m
[7m[0m    sub = tokens[1][0m
[7m[0m    if sub == "test":[0m
[7m[0m        return True, "allow:npm test (verifier policy)"[0m
[7m[0m    if sub == "run" and len(tokens) >= 3:[0m
[7m[0m        script = tokens[2][0m
[7m[0m        if script in {"test", "typecheck", "lint", "check"}:[0m
[7m[0m            return True, f"allow:npm run {script} (verifier policy)"[0m
[7m[0m        return False, f"block:npm run {script} not in verifier allowlist"[0m
[7m[0m    return False, f"block:npm {sub} not in verifier allowlist (test, run test/typecheck/lint/check only)"[0m
[7m[0m
[7m[0m
[7m[0mdef _validate_verifier_cargo(segment: str) -> tuple[bool, str]:[0m
[7m[0m    """Verifier policy: allow `cargo test` + `cargo check`; deny installs/builds."""[0m
[7m[0m    try:[0m
[7m[0m        tokens = shlex.split(segment, posix=False)[0m
[7m[0m    except ValueError:[0m
[7m[0m        return False, "block:cargo command parsing failed"[0m
[7m[0m    if len(tokens) < 2:[0m
[7m[0m        return False, "block:cargo requires subcommand"[0m
[7m[0m    sub = tokens[1][0m
[7m[0m    if sub in {"test", "check"}:[0m
[7m[0m        return True, f"allow:cargo {sub} (verifier policy)"[0m
[7m[0m    return False, f"block:cargo {sub} not in verifier allowlist (test, check only)"[0m
[7m[0m
[7m[0m
[7m[0mdef _validate_verifier_go(segment: str) -> tuple[bool, str]:[0m
[7m[0m    """Verifier policy: allow `go test`; deny everything else (build/get/install)."""[0m
[7m[0m    try:[0m
[7m[0m        tokens = shlex.split(segment, posix=False)[0m
[7m[0m    except ValueError:[0m
[7m[0m        return False, "block:go command parsing failed"[0m
[7m[0m    if len(tokens) < 2:[0m
[7m[0m        return False, "block:go requires subcommand"[0m
[7m[0m    sub = tokens[1][0m
[7m[0m    if sub == "test":[0m
[7m[0m        return True, "allow:go test (verifier policy)"[0m
[7m[0m    return False, f"block:go {sub} not in verifier allowlist (test only)"[0m
[7m[0m
[7m[0m
[7m[0mdef _validate_verifier_tsc(segment: str) -> tuple[bool, str]:[0m
[7m[0m    """Verifier policy: allow `tsc --noEmit` (type-check only); deny emit forms."""[0m
[7m[0m    try:[0m
[7m[0m        tokens = shlex.split(segment, posix=False)[0m
[7m[0m    except ValueError:[0m
[7m[0m        return False, "block:tsc command parsing failed"[0m
[7m[0m    if "--noEmit" in tokens:[0m
[7m[0m        return True, "allow:tsc --noEmit (verifier policy)"[0m
[7m[0m    return False, "block:tsc requires --noEmit under verifier policy (else emits files)"[0m
[7m[0m
[7m[0m
[7m[0mdef _validate_verifier_mypy(segment: str) -> tuple[bool, str]:[0m
[7m[0m    """Verifier policy: allow `mypy` (type-check is read-only by design)."""[0m
[7m[0m    del segment  # mypy is always-allow; segment unused but signature matches sister validators[0m
[7m[0m    return True, "allow:mypy (verifier policy)"[0m
[7m[0m
[7m[0m
[7m[0mdef _validate_segment(segment: str, policy: str) -> tuple[bool, str]:[0m
[7m[0m    """Validate a single command segment per policy."""[0m
[7m[0m    if not segment.strip():[0m
[7m[0m        return True, "allow:empty segment"[0m
[7m[0m    cmd = _first_token(segment)[0m
[7m[0m    if not cmd:[0m
[7m[0m        return False, "block:could not extract command name"[0m
[7m[0m
[7m[0m    # Verifier-policy pre-check for verification tools.[0m
[7m[0m    # Must run BEFORE _ALWAYS_DENY because npm/cargo/go are in always-deny but[0m
[7m[0m    # verifier policy permits specific subcommands (npm test, cargo test/check, go test).[0m
[7m[0m    if policy == "verifier":[0m
[7m[0m        if cmd == "pytest":[0m
[7m[0m            return _validate_verifier_pytest(segment)[0m
[7m[0m        if cmd == "pyright":[0m
[7m[0m            return _validate_verifier_pyright(segment)[0m
[7m[0m        if cmd == "ruff":[0m
[7m[0m            return _validate_verifier_ruff(segment)[0m
[7m[0m        if cmd == "mypy":[0m
[7m[0m            return _validate_verifier_mypy(segment)[0m
[7m[0m        if cmd == "tsc":[0m
[7m[0m            return _validate_verifier_tsc(segment)[0m
[7m[0m        if cmd == "npm":[0m
[7m[0m            return _validate_verifier_npm(segment)[0m
[7m[0m        if cmd == "cargo":[0m
[7m[0m            return _validate_verifier_cargo(segment)[0m
[7m[0m        if cmd == "go":[0m
[7m[0m            return _validate_verifier_go(segment)[0m
[7m[0m
[7m[0m    # Always-deny check (defense-in-depth for non-verifier policies AND[0m
[7m[0m    # for commands not handled by verifier-specific validators)[0m
[7m[0m    if cmd in _ALWAYS_DENY:[0m
[7m[0m        return False, f"block:{cmd} is in always-deny set"[0m
[7m[0m
[7m[0m    # Per-command validators[0m
[7m[0m    if cmd == "git":[0m
[7m[0m        return _validate_git(segment)[0m
[7m[0m    if cmd == "codex":[0m
[7m[0m        return _validate_codex(segment, policy)[0m
[7m[0m    if cmd == "sed":[0m
[7m[0m        return _validate_sed(segment)[0m
[7m[0m    if cmd == "find":[0m
[7m[0m        return _validate_find(segment)[0m
[7m[0m    if cmd == "awk":[0m
[7m[0m        return _validate_awk(segment)[0m
[7m[0m    if cmd in {"python", "python3"}:[0m
[7m[0m        return _validate_python(segment)[0m
[7m[0m
[7m[0m    # Generic readonly allowlist check (verifier inherits readonly allowlist as superset)[0m
[7m[0m    if cmd in _READONLY_COMMANDS:[0m
[7m[0m        return True, f"allow:{cmd} is in readonly allowlist"[0m
[7m[0m
[7m[0m    # Unknown command — fail closed[0m
[7m[0m    return False, f"block:{cmd} not in {policy} allowlist (fail-closed)"[0m
[7m[0m
[7m[0m
[7m[0mdef validate_command(cmd: str, policy: str = "readonly") -> tuple[bool, str]:[0m
[7m[0m    """Sync test-friendly entry point: validate Bash command string against policy.[0m
[7m[0m
[7m[0m    Returns (allowed: bool, reason: str). Reason prefixed with "allow:" or "block:".[0m
[7m[0m    """[0m
[7m[0m    # Stop-hook fix-forward 2026-05-03 — FAIL-CLOSED on shell substitutions[0m
[7m[0m    # BEFORE any segment validation. Substitutions evaluate at exec time and[0m
[7m[0m    # bypass leading-verb allowlists (e.g. `git diff $(rm -rf /)` allowlists[0m
[7m[0m    # `git diff` but inner command runs at substitution time). Probe at[0m
[7m[0m    # tmp/probe_substitution_bypass.py — n=6 distinct bypass shapes confirmed[0m
[7m[0m    # under prior code; all closed by this hard-deny.[0m
[7m[0m    for pat in _SHELL_SUBSTITUTION_DENY_PATTERNS:[0m
[7m[0m        if pat.search(cmd):[0m
[7m[0m            return False, ([0m
[7m[0m                f"block:shell substitution detected ({pat.pattern}) — "[0m
[7m[0m                "read-only mode rejects substitutions; inner command bypasses "[0m
[7m[0m                "leading-verb allowlist (Stop-hook 2026-05-03 fix-forward)"[0m
[7m[0m            )[0m
[7m[0m
[7m[0m    # Newline as command separator — _COMMAND_SEPARATORS regex misses it.[0m
[7m[0m    # Multiline commands sneak destructive segments past per-segment validation.[0m
[7m[0m    if _NEWLINE_SEPARATOR_PATTERN.search(cmd):[0m
[7m[0m        return False, ([0m
[7m[0m            "block:multiline command (newline separator) — read-only mode "[0m
[7m[0m            "rejects; submit one command per Bash invocation"[0m
[7m[0m        )[0m
[7m[0m
[7m[0m    # Check write redirections at full-string level[0m
[7m[0m    if _REDIRECT_WRITE_PATTERN.search(cmd):[0m
[7m[0m        return False, "block:write redirection (> or >>) detected"[0m
[7m[0m    # Split into segments and validate each[0m
[7m[0m    segments = _split_segments(cmd)[0m
[7m[0m    if not segments:[0m
[7m[0m        return False, "block:empty command"[0m
[7m[0m    for seg in segments:[0m
[7m[0m        allowed, reason = _validate_segment(seg, policy)[0m
[7m[0m        if not allowed:[0m
[7m[0m            return False, reason[0m
[7m[0m    return True, "allow:all segments passed"[0m
[7m[0m
[7m[0m
[7m[0mdef main() -> int:[0m
[7m[0m    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)[0m
[7m[0m    parser.add_argument("--policy", default="readonly",[0m
[7m[0m                         choices=["readonly", "codex-readonly", "verifier"],[0m
[7m[0m                         help="Allowlist policy mode (verifier extends readonly with test/type-check tools)")[0m
[7m[0m    args = parser.parse_args()[0m
[7m[0m
[7m[0m    # Env knobs (defense-in-depth precedence)[0m
[7m[0m    if os.environ.get("AGENT_PLAN_BASH_GUARD_DISABLE") == "1":[0m
[7m[0m        return 0[0m
[7m[0m
[7m[0m    # Read PreToolUse JSON from stdin[0m
[7m[0m    try:[0m
[7m[0m        payload = json.load(sys.stdin)[0m
[7m[0m    except json.JSONDecodeError as e:[0m
[7m[0m        print(f"agent_plan_readonly_bash_guard: invalid JSON on stdin: {e}", file=sys.stderr)[0m
[7m[0m        return 0  # Fail open on malformed input (parent CC retries)[0m
[7m[0m
[7m[0m    tool_name = payload.get("tool_name", "")[0m
[7m[0m    if tool_name != "Bash":[0m
[7m[0m        return 0  # Pass-through for non-Bash tools[0m
[7m[0m
[7m[0m    tool_input = payload.get("tool_input") or {}[0m
[7m[0m    cmd = tool_input.get("command", "")[0m
[7m[0m    if not cmd:[0m
